Triggers: "process vs thread", "concurrency", "deadlock", "memory", "garbage collection", "how does it run", "endianness". ([tmthyjw23/Agent-Assets](https://github.com/tmthyjw23/Agent-Assets/tree/HEAD/Skills/SystemDesign/computer-fundamentals))
- **computer-graphics**: Implement transformation matrices, homogeneous coordinates, perspective projection, and frustum culling. ([Mr-Nobody-Anonymous/All-skills](https://github.com/Mr-Nobody-Anonymous/All-skills/tree/HEAD/awesome_skills/graphics/computer-graphics))
- **computer-graphics**: Computer graphics working pack: radiometry and the rendering equation, BRDFs (Lambert, GGX microfacet, Disney/OpenPBR), Monte Carlo path tracing with importance sampling, MIS and Russian roulette, BVH construction and traversal, raster pipeline design (forward+, deferred, visibility buffer, reversed-Z), Vulkan/Metal/WebGPU/DX12 concepts and synchronization, shader languages, color science and tonemapping, geometry processing, SDFs, numerical robustness, and GPU debugging with RenderDoc, Nsight, PIX and Xcode. Load for any rendering, shading, GPU API, color pipeline or mesh-processing task. ([rustcircle/c101](https://github.com/rustcircle/c101/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/computer-graphics))
